# List of minor planets: 8001–9000

The following is a partial [list of minor planets](/wiki/List_of_minor_planets), running from minor-planet number 8001 through 9000, inclusive. The primary data for this and other partial lists is based on [JPL](/wiki/JPL)'s "Small-Body Orbital Elements" and data available from the [Minor Planet Center](/wiki/Minor_Planet_Center). [Critical list](/wiki/Critical-list_minor_planet) information is also provided by the MPC, unless otherwise specified from [Lowell Observatory](/wiki/Lowell_Observatory). A [detailed description](/wiki/List_of_minor_planets#Description_of_partial_lists) of the table's columns and additional sources are given on the main page including a complete [list of every page](/wiki/List_of_minor_planets#Main_index) in this series, and a [statistical break-up](/wiki/List_of_minor_planets#Diagram-breakup-into-populations) on the [dynamical classification](/wiki/List_of_minor_planets#Orbital_groups) of minor planets.

Also see the summary list of all named bodies in [numerical](/wiki/List_of_named_minor_planets_(numerical)) and [alphabetical](/wiki/List_of_named_minor_planets_(alphabetical)) order, and the corresponding [naming citations](/wiki/Meanings_of_minor-planet_names:_8001%E2%80%939000) for the number range of this particular list. New namings may only be added to this list after official publication, as the preannouncement of names is condemned by the [Working Group for Small Bodies Nomenclature](/wiki/Working_Group_for_Small_Bodies_Nomenclature) of the [International Astronomical Union](/wiki/International_Astronomical_Union).
